Beyond providing distortion-robust and cross-view-consistent geometric priors for establishing reliable correspondences in stereo matching, this design boosts training efficiency and accommodates train-test FoV mismatches.Extensive experiments show that our approach achieves higher accuracy than existing methods on out-of-domain datasets and successfully generalizes to real-world consumer camera setups using a single model. Both the model and the dataset will be open-sourced.

</details>


### [100] [MHSA: A Lightweight Framework for Mitigating Hallucinations via Steered Attention in LVLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14966)
*Wei Ding,Yilin Li,Yudong Zhang,Ruobing Xie,Xingwu Sun,Jiansheng Chen,Yu Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MHSA框架，通过训练一个三层MLP生成器来修正跨模态注意力模式，在不修改LVLM参数的情况下有效缓解幻觉现象，将跨模态注意力从幻觉检测扩展到幻觉缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型视觉语言模型(LVLM)在多种多模态任务中表现优异，但依然存在生成内容与视觉输入不一致的幻觉问题。先前工作DHCP仅能从跨模态注意力角度检测幻觉，却无法缓解幻觉。

Method: MHSA训练一个简单的三层MLP生成器来产生修正后的注意力，训练过程由DHCP判别器和LVLM自身的监督信号引导。推理时，仅需将原始跨模态注意力替换为修正后的注意力，无需修改任何LVLM参数。

Result: MHSA在多个数据集和多种LVLM上有效缓解了判别性和生成性幻觉。

Conclusion: MHSA将跨模态注意力机制从幻觉检测扩展到幻觉缓解，为LVLM幻觉研究提供了新视角，有助于提升模型的可靠性。

Abstract: Large vision-language models (LVLMs) have achieved remarkable performance across diverse multimodal tasks, yet they continue to suffer from hallucinations, generating content that is inconsistent with the visual input. Prior work DHCP (Detecting Hallucinations by Cross-modal Attention Pattern) has explored hallucination detection from the perspective of cross-modal attention, but does not address hallucination mitigation. In this paper, we propose MHSA (Mitigating Hallucinations via Steered Attention), a lightweight framework that mitigates hallucinations by learning to correct cross-modal attention patterns in LVLMs. MHSA trains a simple three-layer MLP generator to produce corrected attention, guided by supervisory signals from the DHCP discriminator and the LVLM itself. During inference, MHSA mitigates both discriminative and generative hallucinations across various datasets and LVLMs by simply replacing the original cross-modal attention with the corrected one, without modifying any LVLM parameters. By extending cross-modal attention mechanisms from hallucination detection to hallucination mitigation, MHSA offers a novel perspective on hallucination research in LVLMs and helps enhance their reliability.

</details>


### [101] [MicroscopyMatching: Towards a Ready-to-use Framework for Microscopy Image Analysis in Diverse Conditions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14980)
*Xiaofei Hui,Haoxuan Qu,Hossein Rahmani,Shuohong Wang,Jeff W. Lichtman,Jun Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出MicroscopyMatching，首个即开即用的显微图像分析框架，通过将多样化分析任务统一为匹配问题并利用预训练潜在扩散模型的鲁棒匹配能力，可靠地完成分割、追踪和计数等关键任务，解决了传统方法因显微分析场景多样性而需要大量适配的痛点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前深度学习方法因显微分析场景的多样性（物体类型、处理协议、成像设备、分析任务等差异）需要大量适配工作，给实验室带来难以持续的负担，导致研究者仍依赖耗时费力的人工分析，严重瓶颈了生物医学研究进展；亟需一种可靠且广泛适用的显微图像分析工具。

Method: 从全新角度出发，将分割、追踪、计数等多样化显微分析任务统一归约为一个匹配问题，借助预训练潜在扩散模型（latent diffusion model）的强大匹配能力来解决该统一问题。

Result: 提出了首个即开即用（ready-to-use）的显微图像分析框架MicroscopyMatching，能在多种分析场景下可靠执行分割、追踪和计数等关键任务。

Conclusion: 通过统一任务形式并利用预训练扩散模型的匹配能力，可有效覆盖多样化的显微分析需求，为生物医学研究提供一种可持续、无需大量适配的通用分析工具。

Abstract: Analyzing microscopy images to extract biological object properties (e.g., their morphological organization, temporal dynamics, and population density) is fundamental to various biomedical research. Yet conducting this manually is costly and time-consuming. Though deep learning-based approaches have been explored to automate this process, the substantial diversity of microscopy analysis settings in practice (including variations of biological object types, sample processing protocols, imaging equipment, and analysis tasks, etc.) often renders them ineffective. As a result, these approaches typically require extensive adaptation for different settings, which, however, can impose burdens that are often practically unsustainable for laboratories, forcing biomedical researchers to still commonly rely on manual analysis, thereby severely bottlenecking the pace of biomedical research progress. This situation has created a pressing and long-standing need for a reliable and broadly applicable microscopy image analysis tool, yet such a tool is still missing. To address this gap, we present the first ready-to-use microscopy image analysis framework, MicroscopyMatching, that can reliably perform key analysis tasks (including segmentation, tracking, and counting) across diverse microscopy analysis settings. From a fundamentally different perspective, MicroscopyMatching reformulates diverse microscopy image analysis tasks as a unified matching problem, effectively handling this problem by exploiting the robust matching capability from pre-trained latent diffusion models.

</details>


### [102] [Characterizing the visual representation of objects from the child's view](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14990)
*Jane Yang,Tarun Sepuri,Alvin Wei Ming Tan,Khai Loong Aw,Michael C. Frank,Bria Long*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究通过分析婴儿视角的第一人称视频（BabyView数据集，31名5-36月龄儿童，868小时），使用目标检测模型提取超过300万帧中的常见物体类别，发现儿童视觉经验中物体类别分布高度偏斜（少数类别主导），类别实例高度可变（非典型角度、杂波场景、部分遮挡），且许多类别（尤其是动物）常以描绘形式呈现；尽管如此，检测到的类别在上级类别内呈现强于标准照片的聚类结构，这一模式在自监督视觉和多模态模型及个体儿童的密集采样数据中均得到复现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究婴幼儿在早年日常经验中获取物体类别表征时，学习输入数据（视觉经验）的实际特征是什么。

Method: 使用BabyView数据集中31名5-36月龄儿童的868小时第一人称视频，通过监督式目标检测模型从300多万帧中提取常见物体类别，分析类别分布、实例变异性及上级类别结构，并与标准照片的嵌入空间进行比较。

Result: 物体类别暴露高度偏斜；类别实例高度可变（非典型角度、杂乱场景、遮挡）；许多类别以描绘形式出现；检测类别在上级类别内聚类强于标准照片；该模式在自监督视觉/多模态模型及个体儿童数据中均存在。

Conclusion: 理解视觉类别学习的鲁棒性和效率需要开发能够利用强上级类别结构并从非典型、稀疏、可变的样例中学习的模型。

Abstract: Children acquire object category representations from their everyday experiences in the first few years of life. What do the inputs to this learning process look like? We analyzed first-person videos of young children's visual experience at home from the BabyView dataset ($N$ = 31 participants, 868 hours, ages 5--36 months), using a supervised object detection model to extract common object categories from more than 3 million frames. We found that children's object category exposure was highly skewed: a few categories (e.g., cups, chairs) dominated children's visual experiences while most categories appeared rarely, replicating previous findings from a more restricted set of contexts. Category exemplars were highly variable: children encountered objects from unusual angles, in highly cluttered scenes, and partially occluded views; many categories (especially animals) were most frequently viewed as depictions. Surprisingly, despite this variability, detected categories (e.g., giraffes, apples) showed stronger groupings within superordinate categories (e.g., animals, food) relative to groupings derived from canonical photographs of these categories. We found this same pattern when using high-dimensional embeddings from both self-supervised visual and multimodal models; this effect was also recapitulated in densely sampled data from individual children. Understanding the robustness and efficiency of visual category learning will require the development of models that can exploit strong superordinate structure and learn from non-canonical, sparse, and variable exemplars.

</details>


### [103] [Predicting Response to Neoadjuvant Chemotherapy in Ovarian Cancer from CT Baseline Using Multi-Loss Deep Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14991)
*Francesco Pastori,Francesca Fati,Marina Rosanu,Luigi De Vitis,Lucia Ribero,Gabriella Schivardi,Giovanni Damiano Aletti,Nicoletta Colombo,Jvan Casarin,Francesco Multinu,Elena De Momi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种基于深度学习的方法，利用治疗前的增强CT和自动生成的3D病灶掩膜预测卵巢癌患者对新辅助化疗的反应，在测试集上达到了0.73的ROC-AUC和0.70的F1分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卵巢癌是最致命的妇科恶性肿瘤，约60%患者在晚期确诊，5年生存率约30%。早期识别对新辅助化疗无反应的患者可以避免无效治疗并防止延误最佳手术时机，这是一个关键的未满足临床需求。

Method: 构建了一个非侵入性深度学习框架：使用预训练的图像编码器（部分微调）编码轴向切片，通过注意力模块将切片级表示聚合为体积嵌入，训练结合分类损失、有监督对比正则化和难负样本挖掘。

Result: 在来自欧洲肿瘤研究所（米兰）的280例患者（147例有反应，133例无反应）的回顾性单中心队列上，测试集ROC-AUC为0.73（95% CI: 0.58-0.86），F1分数为0.70（95% CI: 0.56-0.82）。

Conclusion: 提出的架构能够学习临床相关的预测模式，为基于影像学的分层工具提供了坚实基础，有望实现治疗前的化疗反应预测。

Abstract: Ovarian cancer is the most lethal gynecologic malignancy: around 60% of patients are diagnosed at an advanced stage, with an associated 5-year survival rate of about 30%. Early identification of non-responders to neoadjuvant chemotherapy remains a key unmet need, as it could prevent ineffective therapy and avoid delays in optimal surgical management. This work proposes a non-invasive deep learning framework to predict neoadjuvant chemotherapy response from pre-treatment contrast-enhanced CT by leveraging automatically derived 3D lesion masks. The approach encodes axial slices with a partially fine-tuned pretrained image encoder and aggregates slice-level representations into a volumetric embedding through an attention-based module. Training combines classification loss with supervised contrastive regularization and hard-negative mining to improve separation between ambiguous responders and non-responders. The method was developed on a retrospective single-center cohort from the European Institute of Oncology (Milan, IT), including 280 eligible patients (147 responder, 133 non-responder). On the test cohort, the model achieved a ROC-AUC of 0.73 (95% CI: 0.58-0.86) and an F1-score of 0.70 (95% CI: 0.56-0.82). Overall, these results suggest that the proposed architecture learns clinically relevant predictive patterns and provides a robust foundation for an imaging-based stratification tool.

</details>


### [104] [3D Skew-Normal Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15010)
*Xiangru Wu,Ke Fan,Yanwei Fu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出 Skew-Normal Splatting (SNS)，采用阿萨利尼偏态正态分布作为基本图元，通过可学习的偏度参数实现从对称高斯到半高斯形状的连续插值，在保持解析可跟踪性的同时提升了边界和不对称结构的重建质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3DGS在有限图元预算下，对称形状的基本图元在表示不对称结构（如物体边界、单侧表面）时紧凑性不足；现有工作要么局限于椭圆族分布，要么依赖硬截断，限制了连续形状控制并引入分布不连续性。

Method: 采用Azzalini Skew-Normal分布作为基础图元，引入可学习的有限偏度参数实现形状连续插值；提出解耦参数化和块级优化策略应对尺度、旋转与偏度参数的强耦合问题；保持仿射变换和边缘化的解析可跟踪性以无缝集成现有光栅化管线。

Result: 在标准新视角合成基准上，SNS在重建质量上持续优于高斯及近期非高斯核方法，在锐利边界、薄结构和单侧结构上优势尤为明显。

Conclusion: 偏态正态分布作为3DGS基本图元可有效提升表示紧凑性和重建精度，为神经渲染中的非对称核设计提供了新方向。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) has emerged as a leading representation for real-time novel view synthesis and been widely adopted in various downstream applications. The core strength of 3DGS lies in its efficient kernel-based scene representation, where Gaussian primitives provide favorable mathematical and computational properties. However, under a finite primitive budget, the symmetric shape of each primitive directly affects representation compactness, especially near asymmetric structures such as object boundaries and one-sided surfaces. Recent works have explored more complex kernel distributions, yet they either remain within the elliptical family or rely on hard truncation, which limits continuous shape control and introduces distributional discontinuities. In this paper, we propose Skew-Normal Splatting (SNS), which adopts the Azzalini Skew-Normal distribution as the fundamental primitive. By introducing a learnable and bounded skewness parameter, SNS can continuously interpolate between symmetric Gaussians and Half-Gaussian-like shapes, enabling flexible modeling of both sharp boundaries and interior regions. Moremover, SNS preserves analytical tractability under affine transformations and marginalization. This property allows seamless integration into existing Gaussian Splatting rasterization pipelines.Furthermore, to address the strong coupling between scale, rotation, and skewness parameters, we introduce a decoupled parameterization and a block-wise optimization strategy to enhance training stability and accuracy. Extensive experiments on standard novel-view synthesis benchmarks show that SNS consistently improves reconstruction quality over Gaussian and recent non-Gaussian kernels, with clearer benefits on sharp boundaries and thin or one-sided structures.

</details>


### [105] [EverAnimate: Minute-Scale Human Animation via Latent Flow Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15042)
*Wuyang Li,Yang Gao,Mariam Hassan,Lan Feng,Wentao Pan,Po-Chien Luan,Alexandre Alahi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EverAnimate提出了一种高效的后训练方法，通过持久化潜在上下文记忆（包括持久化潜在传播和修复性流匹配）解决长时动画视频生成中的质量漂移和语义漂移问题，在10秒和90秒生成任务中均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长时动画生成面临挑战：高度动态的人体运动需在相对静态的环境中合成，基于分块的生成方法容易产生累积漂移，包括低层质量漂移（如静态背景逐步退化）和高层语义漂移（如角色身份和视角相关属性不一致）。

Method: EverAnimate包含两种互补机制：(i) 持久化潜在传播——跨分块维护上下文记忆，在潜在空间中传播身份与运动，缓解时序遗忘；(ii) 修复性流匹配——通过速度调整引入隐式修复目标，提升分块内保真度。仅需轻量级LoRA微调。

Result: 在10秒生成中，PSNR/SSIM提升8%/7%，LPIPS/FID降低22%/11%；在90秒生成中，PSNR/SSIM提升15%/15%，LPIPS/FID降低32%/27%，全面超越SOTA长动画方法。

Conclusion: EverAnimate通过持久化潜在上下文记忆高效解决了长时动画生成中的累积漂移问题，仅需少量微调即可在短时和长时设定下取得显著性能提升。

Abstract: We propose EverAnimate, an efficient post-training method for long-horizon animated video generation that preserves visual quality and character identity. Long-form animation remains challenging because highly dynamic human motion must be synthesized against relatively static environments, making chunk-based generation prone to accumulated drift: (i) low-level quality drift, such as progressive degradation of static backgrounds, and (ii) high-level semantic drift, such as inconsistent character identity and view-dependent attributes. To address this issue, EverAnimate restores drifted flow trajectories by anchoring generation to a persistent latent context memory, consisting of two complementary mechanisms. (i) Persistent Latent Propagation maintains a context memory across chunks to propagate identity and motion in latent space while mitigating temporal forgetting. (ii) Restorative Flow Matching introduces an implicit restoration objective during sampling through velocity adjustment, improving within-chunk fidelity. With only lightweight LoRA tuning, EverAnimate outperforms state-of-the-art long-animation methods in both short- and long-horizon settings: at 10 seconds, it improves PSNR/SSIM by 8%/7% and reduces LPIPS/FID by 22%/11%; at 90 seconds, the gains increase to 15%/15% and 32%/27%, respectively.

</details>


### [106] [LATERN: Test-Time Context-Aware Explainable Video Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15054)
*Mitchell Piehl,Muchao Ye*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: LATERN提出了一种基于视觉语言模型的视频异常检测框架，通过上下文感知的时间证据聚合机制，实现了更准确、时间连贯且语义可解释的异常检测与事件级解释。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于VLM的视频异常检测方法由于token限制，只能独立进行片段级推理，缺乏结构化时间上下文，导致预测和解释碎片化。本文旨在让VLM能感知视频动态演化中的异常偏差，而非孤立地处理每个片段。

Method: 提出LATERN框架，包含两个互补模块：1) Context-Aware Anomaly Scoring (CEA)，通过基于帧多样性和视觉-文本对齐的图像引导记忆机制选择历史内容作为扩展上下文，生成可靠异常分数；2) Recursive Evidence Aggregation (REA)，对异常分数进行递归时间聚合，识别连贯的异常区间，生成基于视觉-文本证据的事件级决策和解释。

Result: 在UCF-Crime和XD-Violence基准上，LATERN在测试时提升了冻结VLM的检测精度和解释一致性，生成了时间连贯且语义可解释的事件级解释。

Conclusion: LATERN通过将VAD重新表述为时间证据聚合过程，有效解决了VLM在VAD中缺乏时空上下文的局限性，实现了更优的异常检测性能和可解释性。

Abstract: Vision-language models (VLMs) have recently emerged as a promising paradigm for video anomaly detection (VAD) due to their strong visual reasoning ability and natural language-based explainability. In this paper, we aim to address a key limitation of such pipelines, which perform segment-level inference independently owing to token constraints and reason without structured temporal context, allowing VLMs to interpret anomalies as deviations from evolving video dynamics rather than producing fragmented predictions and explanations. To specify, we propose a context-aware framework named LATERN, which reformulates VAD as a temporal evidence aggregation process. LATERN consists of two complementary modules: Context-Aware Anomaly Scoring (CEA) and Recursive Evidence Aggregation (REA). CEA introduces a novel image-grounded memory mechanism, which selectively chooses historical content via frame diversity and visual-textual alignment as expanded context to help generate reliable anomaly scores. Building upon these scores, REA performs recursive temporal aggregation to identify coherent anomaly intervals and produce event-level decisions and explanations grounded in visual-textual evidence. Extensive experiments on challenging benchmarks, including UCF-Crime and XD-Violence, show that LATERN enhances detection accuracy and explanation consistency for frozen VLMs during test time, while generating temporally coherent and semantically grounded event-level explanations.

</details>


### [107] [CoralLite: μCT Reconstruction of Coral Colonies from Individual Corallites](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15093)
*Jess Jones,Leonardo Bertini,Kenneth Johnson,Erica Hendy,Tilo Burghardt*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出CoralLite数据集和混合V-Trans-UNet架构，首次实现从μCT扫描中自动分割和3D重建珊瑚个体珊瑚杯（corallite），在未见切片上达到0.94拓扑准确率和0.77 Dice分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 珊瑚的生命历史记录在骨骼中，但科学家缺乏追踪每个珊瑚杯沉积速率和时间的方法，需要自动化的深度学习工具来支持大规模、高精度的珊瑚个体结构分析。

Method: 构建CoralLite数据集（697张μCT切片、37张部分或完整标注），提出混合V-Trans-UNet架构，先在弱标注数据上预训练，再使用8000+手动标注的全标注切片进行拓扑感知微调，实现tiled虚拟薄片分割和跨切片链接的3D重建。

Result: 在同一菌落的未见切片上达到0.94拓扑准确率和0.77平均Dice分数；在不同菌落上达到0.63平均Dice分数。首次证明视觉机器学习可有效支持从μCT扫描进行全3D单个珊瑚杯建模。

Conclusion: 该工作为珊瑚骨骼生长研究提供了首个深度学习基准，数据集和代码已公开以促进可重复性和后续研究。

Abstract: The life history of an individual coral is archived within the accreting skeleton of the colony. While reef-forming coral colonies (e.g. massive \emph{Porites} sp.) may live for hundreds of years and deposit calcareous structures many metres in height and width, their living tissue is a thin outer surface layer comprised of asexually-dividing polyps that only survive a few years. To understand the rate and timing of polyp division and the consequences for colony skeletal growth, scientists need to track the skeletal corallite deposited around each polyp. Here we propose CoralLite, an annotated μCT scan dataset of entire calcareous skeletons and an associated, first corallite deep learning reconstruction baseline. CoralLite combines fully quantified volumetric segmentations with cross-slice linking for visualisations of 3D models for each corallite up to colony scale. For segmentation, we propose and evaluate in detail a hybrid V-Trans-UNet architecture applicable to segmenting tiled μCT virtual slabs of \emph{Porites} sp. colonies. The model is pre-trained on weakly annotated data and topology-aware fine-tuned using fully annotated slice sections with 8k+ manual corallite region annotations. On unseen slices of the same colony, the resulting model reaches 0.94 topological accuracy at mean Dice scores of 0.77 on the same colony and projection axis, and 0.63 mean Dice scores on a different, biologically unrelated specimen. Whilst our experiments are limited in scale and context, our results show for the first time that visual machine learning can effectively support full 3D individual corallite modelling from μCT scans of coral skeletons alone. For reproducibility and as a baseline for future research we publish our full dataset of 697 μCT slices, 37 partial or full slice annotations, and all network weights and source code with this paper.

</details>


### [108] [DriveCtrl: Conditioned Sim-to-Real Driving Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15116)
*Haonan Zhao,Yiting Wang,Jingkun Chen,Valentina Donzella,Thomas Bashford-Rogers,Kurt Debattista*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DriveCtrl，一种基于深度条件的可控仿真到真实视频生成框架，用于合成逼真的驾驶视频。该方法在预训练视频基础模型上引入结构感知适配器，实现深度引导生成，同时保留源仿真的场景布局和运动模式，生成时序一致的驾驶视频。作者还提出了可扩展的数据生成管道和新的评估指标DVRS，实验表明DriveCtrl在真实性、时序质量和感知任务性能上均优于基线方法，显著缩小了仿真到真实的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模标注的驾驶视频数据对训练自动驾驶系统至关重要，但仿真数据与真实驾驶视频之间存在领域差异，限制了其在下游部署中的效用。现有视频生成方法无法同时保持场景结构、物体动态、时序一致性和视觉逼真度，而这些对于保持生成数据中标注的有效性至关重要。

Method: 基于预训练视频基础模型，引入结构感知适配器实现深度引导生成；提出可扩展的数据生成管道，支持深度、参考数据集风格和文本提示三种条件信号；提出驾驶领域知识驱动的评估指标DVRS。

Result: DriveCtrl在真实性、时序质量和感知任务性能上持续优于基础模型和竞争替代方案，显著缩小了驾驶视频生成的仿真到真实差距。

Conclusion: DriveCtrl通过深度条件可控生成有效弥合了仿真与真实驾驶视频之间的领域差异，为自动驾驶系统训练提供了高质量的可扩展数据生成方案。

Abstract: Large-scale labelled driving video data is essential for training autonomous driving systems. Although simulation offers scalable and fully annotated data, the domain gap between synthetic and real-world driving videos significantly limits its utility for downstream deployment. Existing video generation methods are not well-suited for this task, as they fail to simultaneously preserve scene structure, object dynamics, temporal consistency, and visual realism, all of which are critical for maintaining annotation validity in generated data. In this paper, we present DriveCtrl, a depth-conditioned controllable sim-to-real video generation framework for realistic driving video synthesis. Built upon a pretrained video foundation model, DriveCtrl introduces a structure-aware adapter that enables depth-guided generation while preserving the scene layout and motion patterns of the source simulation, producing temporally coherent driving videos that remain aligned with the original simulated sequences. We further introduce a scalable data generation pipeline that transforms simulator videos into realistic driving footage matching the visual style of a target real-world dataset. The pipeline supports three conditioning signals: structural depth, reference-dataset style, and text prompts, while preserving frame-level annotations for downstream perception tasks. To better assess this task, we propose a driving-domain-specific knowledge-informed evaluation metric called Driving Video Realism Score (DVRS) that assesses the realism of generated videos. Experiments demonstrate that DriveCtrl consistently outperforms the base model and competing alternatives in realism, temporal quality, and perception task performance, substantially narrowing the sim-to-real gap for driving video generation.

</details>


### [109] [Causal Forcing++: Scalable Few-Step Autoregressive Diffusion Distillation for Real-Time Interactive Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15141)
*Min Zhao,Hongzhou Zhu,Kaiwen Zheng,Zihan Zhou,Bokai Yan,Xinyuan Li,Xiao Yang,Chongxuan Li,Jun Zhu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出Causal Forcing++，一种用于帧级自回归视频生成的因果一致性蒸馏（causal CD）初始化方法。该方法在仅1-2步采样的帧级自回归设定下，通过因果一致性蒸馏学习AR条件流映射，避免预计算完整PF-ODE轨迹，在VBench Total、Quality和VisionReward上分别超越SOTA的4步块级因果强制方法0.1、0.3和0.335，同时首帧延迟降低50%，阶段二训练成本减少约4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AR扩散蒸馏方法在块级4步设定下表现良好，但存在响应粒度过粗和采样延迟不可忽略的问题。本文研究更激进的帧级自回归（仅1-2步采样）设定，发现少步AR学生的初始化是关键瓶颈：现有策略要么目标不对齐、无法支持少步生成，要么扩展成本过高。

Method: 提出Causal Forcing++管道，核心是因果一致性蒸馏（causal CD）：通过在线教师ODE单步（相邻时间步之间）提供监督，学习与因果ODE蒸馏相同的AR条件流映射，无需预计算和存储完整PF-ODE轨迹，使得初始化更高效且更易优化。

Result: 在帧级2步设定下，VBench Total提升0.1，VBench Quality提升0.3，VisionReward提升0.335；首帧延迟降低50%，Stage 2训练成本减少约4倍；并扩展至Genie3风格的基于动作条件的生成。

Conclusion: Causal Forcing++通过因果一致性蒸馏有效解决了帧级少步自回归视频生成的初始化瓶颈，实现了更精细的生成粒度、更低的延迟和更高效的训练，并在多种设定下达到SOTA性能。

Abstract: Real-time interactive video generation requires low-latency, streaming, and controllable rollout. Existing autoregressive (AR) diffusion distillation methods have achieved strong results in the chunk-wise 4-step regime by distilling bidirectional base models into few-step AR students, but they remain limited by coarse response granularity and non-negligible sampling latency. In this paper, we study a more aggressive setting: frame-wise autoregression with only 1--2 sampling steps. In this regime, we identify the initialization of a few-step AR student as the key bottleneck: existing strategies are either target-misaligned, incapable of few-step generation, or too costly to scale. We propose \textbf{Causal Forcing++}, a principled and scalable pipeline that uses \emph{causal consistency distillation} (causal CD) for few-step AR initialization. The core idea is that causal CD learns the same AR-conditional flow map as causal ODE distillation, but obtains supervision from a single online teacher ODE step between adjacent timesteps, avoiding the need to precompute and store full PF-ODE trajectories. This makes the initialization both more efficient and easier to optimize. The resulting pipeline, \ours, surpasses the SOTA 4-step chunk-wise Causal Forcing under the \textit{\textbf{frame-wise 2-step setting}} by 0.1 in VBench Total, 0.3 in VBench Quality, and 0.335 in VisionReward, while reducing first-frame latency by 50\% and Stage 2 training cost by $\sim$$4\times$. We further extend the pipeline to action-conditioned world model generation in the spirit of Genie3. Project Page: https://github.com/thu-ml/Causal-Forcing and https://github.com/shengshu-ai/minWM .

</details>


### [110] [Does Synthetic Layered Design Data Benefit Layered Design Decomposition?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15167)
*Kam Man Wu,Haolin Yang,Qingyu Chen,Yihu Tang,Jingye Chen,Qifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了纯合成分层数据在图形设计分解中的有效性。基于CLD基线，构建了SynLayers合成数据集，利用视觉语言模型生成文本监督并自动推理输入边界框。实验发现：(1)纯合成数据训练可超越真实数据集PrismLayersPro；(2)性能随数据规模提升，约50K样本后趋于饱和；(3)合成数据能避免真实数据中的图层数量不平衡问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像生成技术虽能产生高质量图像，但输出是"压平"的，前景、背景和文本被固定在单一画布中，导致生成后编辑困难，存在可用性的"最后一公里"问题。现有方法依赖稀缺的商业分层资产或构建部分合成数据，均面临可扩展性挑战。

Method: 基于CLD（最先进的图层分解框架）进行数据驱动研究。构建纯合成数据集SynLayers，使用视觉语言模型（VLM）生成文本监督，并用VLM预测的边界框自动化推理输入。假设图形设计中图层分解不需要精确建模层间依赖关系，因为设计元素通常是模块化且语义可分离的。

Result: (1)纯合成数据训练效果优于不可扩展的替代方案（如PrismLayersPro数据集）；(2)性能随数据规模增加而提升，但在约50K样本时开始饱和；(3)合成数据可对图层数量分布进行平衡控制，避免真实数据中常见的图层数量不平衡问题。

Conclusion: 纯合成分层数据可作为图形设计编辑系统的实用且可扩展的基础，鼓励更广泛地采用合成数据来解决图形设计分解中的可扩展性问题。

Abstract: Recent advances in image generation have made it easy to produce high-quality images. However, these outputs are inherently flattened, entangling foreground elements, background, and text within a fixed canvas. As a result, flexible post-generation editing remains challenging, revealing a clear last-mile gap toward practical usability. Existing approaches either rely on scarce proprietary layered assets or construct partially synthetic data from limited structural priors. However, both strategies face fundamental challenges in scalability. In this work, we investigate whether pure synthetic layered data can improve graphic design decomposition. We make the assumption that, in graphic design, effective decomposition does not require modeling inter-layer dependencies as precisely as in natural-image composition, since design elements are often intentionally arranged as modular and semantically separable components. Concretely, we conduct a data-centric study based on CLD baseline, which is a state-of-the-art layer decomposition framework. Based on the baseline, we construct our own synthetic dataset, SynLayers, generate textual supervision using vision language models, and automate inference inputs with VLM-predicted bounding boxes. Our study reveals three key findings: (1) even training with purely synthetic data can outperform non-scalable alternatives such as the widely used PrismLayersPro dataset, demonstrating its viability as a scalable and effective substitute; (2) performance consistently improves with increased training data scale, while gains begin to saturate at around 50K samples; and (3) synthetic data enables balanced control over layer-count distributions, avoiding the layer-count imbalance commonly observed in real-world datasets. We hope this data-centric study encourages broader adoption of synthetic data as a practical foundation for layered design editing systems.

</details>


### [111] [Evidential Reasoning Advances Interpretable Real-World Disease Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15171)
*Chenyu Lian,Hong-Yu Zhou,Jing Qin*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EviScreen是一个基于证据推理的疾病筛查框架，通过从双知识库检索区域性历史病例证据，结合证据感知推理模块和对比检索生成的异常图，实现了可追溯解释性和定位可解释性，在临床级召回率下取得了更高的特异性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学图像筛查模型可解释性有限、性能欠佳，缺乏引用历史病例的机制和透明的推理路径。

Method: 构建双知识库存储历史病例的区域级证据；通过对比检索从历史病例中获取相关证据；证据感知推理模块结合当前病例与历史证据进行预测；利用对比检索生成异常图实现定位可解释性。

Result: 在真实疾病筛查基准上取得优越性能，在临床级召回率下实现显著更高的特异性。

Conclusion: EviScreen通过双知识库证据检索和证据感知推理，同时提升了疾病筛查的可解释性和性能。

Abstract: Disease screening is critical for early detection and timely intervention in clinical practice. However, most current screening models for medical images suffer from limited interpretability and suboptimal performance. They often lack effective mechanisms to reference historical cases or provide transparent reasoning pathways. To address these challenges, we introduce EviScreen, an evidential reasoning framework for disease screening that leverages region-level evidence from historical cases. The proposed EviScreen offers retrospection interpretability through regional evidence retrieved from dual knowledge banks. Using this evidential mechanism, the subsequent evidence-aware reasoning module makes predictions using both the current case and evidence from historical cases, thereby enhancing disease screening performance. Furthermore, rather than relying on post-hoc saliency maps, EviScreen enhances localization interpretability by leveraging abnormality maps derived from contrastive retrieval. Our method achieves superior performance on our carefully established benchmarks for real-world disease screening, yielding notably higher specificity at clinical-level recall. Code is publicly available at https://github.com/DopamineLcy/EviScreen.

</details>


### [112] [SANA-WM: Efficient Minute-Scale World Modeling with Hybrid Linear Diffusion Transformer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15178)
*Haoyi Zhu,Haozhe Liu,Yuyang Zhao,Tian Ye,Junsong Chen,Jincheng Yu,Tong He,Song Han,Enze Xie*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SANA-WM是一个2.6B参数的开源世界模型，原生支持一分钟长视频生成，通过混合线性注意力、双分支相机控制、两阶段生成流程和鲁棒标注管线四大核心设计，在仅使用约21.3万公开视频片段、64块H100训练15天的情况下，实现了720p/60秒视频的高质量生成，视觉质量媲美大规模工业基线，推理吞吐量提升36倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源世界模型在长时程高保真视频生成方面存在不足，尤其是分钟级视频生成和精确相机控制能力有限，且训练和推理效率低下。作者旨在构建一个高效、高质量、支持精确相机控制的分钟级开源世界模型。

Method: (1) 混合线性注意力：帧级Gated DeltaNet结合softmax注意力，实现内存高效的长上下文建模；(2) 双分支相机控制：确保精确的6自由度轨迹跟随；(3) 两阶段生成流程：第一阶段生成后使用长视频精炼器提升质量和一致性；(4) 鲁棒标注管线：从公开视频中提取精确的度量尺度6-DoF相机位姿，生成时空一致的动作标签。

Result: 仅用约21.3万公开视频片段和度量尺度位姿监督，在64块H100上训练15天；单GPU即可生成60秒720p视频；蒸馏变体结合NVFP4量化可在单张RTX 5090上34秒完成去噪；在分钟级世界模型基准上动作跟随准确率优于开源基线，视觉质量相当但吞吐量提升36倍。

Conclusion: SANA-WM证明了在有限数据和计算资源下，通过精心设计的架构和流程，可以训练出媲美大规模工业基线的高效分钟级世界模型，为可扩展的世界建模提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: We introduce SANA-WM, an efficient 2.6B-parameter open-source world model natively trained for one-minute generation, synthesizing high-fidelity, 720p, minute-scale videos with precise camera control. SANA-WM achieves visual quality comparable to large-scale industrial baselines such as LingBot-World and HY-WorldPlay, while significantly improving efficiency. Four core designs drive our architecture: (1) Hybrid Linear Attention combines frame-wise Gated DeltaNet (GDN) with softmax attention for memory-efficient long-context modeling. (2) Dual-Branch Camera Control ensures precise 6-DoF trajectory adherence. (3) Two-Stage Generation Pipeline applies a long-video refiner to stage-1 outputs, improving quality and consistency across sequences. (4) Robust Annotation Pipeline extracts accurate metric-scale 6-DoF camera poses from public videos to yield high-quality, spatiotemporally consistent action labels. Driven by these designs, SANA-WMdemonstrates remarkable efficiency across data, training compute, and inference hardware: it uses only $\sim$213K public video clips with metric-scale pose supervision, completes training in 15 days on 64 H100s, and generates each 60s clip on a single GPU; its distilled variant can be deployed on a single RTX 5090 with NVFP4 quantization to denoise a 60s 720p clip in 34s. On our one-minute world-model benchmark, SANA-WM demonstrates stronger action-following accuracy than prior open-source baselines and achieves comparable visual quality at $36\times$ higher throughput for scalable world modeling.

</details>


### [113] [From Plans to Pixels: Learning to Plan and Orchestrate for Open-Ended Image Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15181)
*Anirudh Sundara Rajan,Krishna Kumar Singh,Yong Jae Lee*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出一种经验驱动框架，通过规划器生成结构化原子分解、编排器选择工具和区域逐步执行，并利用视觉语言评判器提供基于结果的奖励，训练编排器最大化奖励并用成功轨迹优化规划器，实现长跨度图像编辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像编辑模型在处理抽象、多步骤指令时效果不佳，而基于智能体的方法依赖手工流水线或教师模仿，缺乏灵活性与编辑结果反馈的耦合。

Method: 提出经验驱动框架：规划器生成结构化原子分解，编排器选择工具和区域执行每一步，视觉语言评判器提供基于结果的奖励以评估指令遵循度和视觉质量，编排器通过最大化奖励进行训练，成功轨迹用于优化规划器。

Result: 该方法相比单步或基于规则的多步骤基线方法，生成了更连贯、更可靠的编辑结果。

Conclusion: 将规划与奖励驱动的执行紧密耦合，能够产生比现有方法更连贯可靠的编辑效果。

Abstract: Modern image editing models produce realistic results but struggle with abstract, multi step instructions (e.g., ``make this advertisement more vegetarian-friendly''). Prior agent based methods decompose such tasks but rely on handcrafted pipelines or teacher imitation, limiting flexibility and decoupling learning from actual editing outcomes. We propose an experiential framework for long-horizon image editing, where a planner generates structured atomic decompositions and an orchestrator selects tools and regions to execute each step. A vision language judge provides outcome-based rewards for instruction adherence and visual quality. The orchestrator is trained to maximize these rewards, and successful trajectories are used to refine the planner. By tightly coupling planning with reward driven execution, our approach yields more coherent and reliable edits than single-step or rule-based multistep baselines.

</details>


### [114] [Warp-as-History: Generalizable Camera-Controlled Video Generation from One Training Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15182)
*Yifan Wang,Tong He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出Warp-as-History接口，通过将相机运动导致的图像变形作为伪历史帧输入到冻结的视频生成模型中，无需训练或测试时优化即可实现零样本的相机控制视频生成，轻量级LoRA微调可进一步提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有相机控制视频生成方法通常需要在大规模带相机标注的视频上进行后训练，或需要测试时优化/额外去噪引导，计算成本高；本文旨在探索一种无需训练、无需架构修改、无需测试时优化的零样本相机控制方案。

Method: 提出Warp-as-History接口：(1) 根据目标相机轨迹，对历史观测帧进行相机变形得到伪历史帧；(2) 将伪历史帧与目标帧的位置编码对齐；(3) 移除无效源观测的变形历史token。该接口将变形后的伪历史通过模型的视觉历史通路输入。还提出轻量级离线LoRA微调方案，仅用单个相机标注视频即可提升性能。

Result: 在多个数据集上的实验表明，该方法实现了零样本的相机轨迹跟随能力；轻量级LoRA微调进一步提升了相机一致性、视觉质量和运动动态，且无需测试时优化或目标视频适配。

Conclusion: Warp-as-History是一种简单有效的接口，揭示了冻结视频生成模型具备非平凡的零样本相机控制能力，轻量级微调可进一步增强该能力，为相机控制视频生成提供了一种低成本的替代方案。

Abstract: Camera-controlled video generation has made substantial progress, enabling generated videos to follow prescribed viewpoint trajectories. However, existing methods usually learn camera-specific conditioning through camera encoders, control branches, or attention and positional-encoding modifications, which often require post-training on large-scale camera-annotated videos. Training-free alternatives avoid such post-training, but often shift the cost to test-time optimization or extra denoising-time guidance. We propose Warp-as-History, a simple interface that turns camera-induced warps into camera-warped pseudo-history with target-frame positional alignment and visible-token selection. Given a target camera trajectory, we construct camera-warped pseudo-history from past observations and feed it through the model's visual-history pathway. Crucially, we align its positional encoding with the target frames being denoised and remove warped-history tokens without valid source observations. Without any training, architectural modification, or test-time optimization, this interface reveals a non-trivial zero-shot capability of a frozen video generation model to follow camera trajectories. Moreover, lightweight offline LoRA finetuning on only one camera-annotated video further improves this capability and generalizes to unseen videos, improving camera adherence, visual quality, and motion dynamics without test-time optimization or target-video adaptation. Extensive experiments on diverse datasets confirm the effectiveness of our method.

</details>


### [115] [Quantitative Video World Model Evaluation for Geometric-Consistency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15185)
*Jiaxin Wu,Yihao Pi,Yinling Zhang,Yuheng Li,Xueyan Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出PDI-Bench（视角失真指标），一个用于评估生成视频中几何一致性的定量框架，通过分割、点跟踪和3D重建计算三个维度的几何残差，揭示现有视频生成器在物理合理性方面的系统性缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成视频评估方法过度依赖人类判断或学习型评分器，具有主观性且难以诊断几何层面的失败模式，缺乏能够定量审计生成视频中3D结构和运动物理合理性的自动化工具。

Method: 利用SAM 2、MegaSaM和CoTracker3进行分割和点跟踪，获取物体级观测；通过单目重建将其提升到3D世界坐标；计算投影几何残差，涵盖尺度-深度对齐、3D运动一致性和3D结构刚性三个失败维度。

Result: 在多种最新视频生成器上，PDI揭示了常见的几何特定失败模式，这些模式无法被常见的感知指标捕获，并提供可用于追踪朝向物理合理视频生成进展的诊断信号。

Conclusion: PDI-Bench为评估生成视频的物理世界建模能力提供了有效的定量诊断工具，填补了现有评估指标在几何一致性方面的空白，有助于推动物理驱动视频生成和世界模型的发展。

Abstract: Generative video models are increasingly studied as implicit world models, yet evaluating whether they produce physically plausible 3D structure and motion remains challenging. Most existing video evaluation pipelines rely heavily on human judgment or learned graders, which can be subjective and weakly diagnostic for geometric failures. We introduce PDI-Bench (Perspective Distortion Index), a quantitative framework for auditing geometric coherence in generated videos. Given a generated clip, we obtain object-centric observations via segmentation and point tracking (e.g., SAM 2, MegaSaM, and CoTracker3), lift them to 3D world-space coordinates via monocular reconstruction, and compute a set of projective-geometry residuals capturing three failure dimensions: scale-depth alignment, 3D motion consistency, and 3D structural rigidity. To support systematic evaluation, we build PDI-Dataset, covering diverse scenarios designed to stress these geometric constraints. Across state-of-the-art video generators, PDI reveals consistent geometry-specific failure modes that are not captured by common perceptual metrics, and provides a diagnostic signal for progress toward physically grounded video generation and physical world model. Our code and dataset can be found at https://pdi-bench.github.io/.

</details>


### [116] [VGGT-Edit: Feed-forward Native 3D Scene Editing with Residual Field Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15186)
*Kaixin Zhu,Yiwen Tang,Yifan Yang,Renrui Zhang,Bohan Zeng,Ziyu Guo,Ruichuan An,Zhou Liu,Qizhi Chen,Delin Qu,Jaehong Yoon,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出VGGT-Edit，一种前馈式3D场景编辑框架，通过深度同步文本注入和残差变换头实现原生3D编辑，显著优于2D-lifting方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前馈式3D重建模型在静态场景感知上表现优异，但无法响应动态人类指令；现有编辑方法依赖2D-lifting策略，导致模糊纹理和几何不一致问题。

Method: 提出VGGT-Edit框架，包含深度同步文本注入（对齐语义引导与空间姿态）和残差变换头（直接预测3D几何位移以变形场景，同时保持背景稳定），并使用多目标损失函数监督训练，同时构建了DeltaScene大规模数据集。

Result: 在多个指标上显著优于2D-lifting基线，生成更锐利的物体细节、更强的多视图一致性，并实现近即时推理速度。

Conclusion: VGGT-Edit通过原生3D编辑方案有效解决了2D-lifting策略的固有问题，为交互式3D应用提供了高效且高质量的解决方案。

Abstract: High-quality 3D scene reconstruction has recently advanced toward generalizable feed-forward architectures, enabling the generation of complex environments in a single forward pass. However, despite their strong performance in static scene perception, these models remain limited in responding to dynamic human instructions, which restricts their use in interactive applications. Existing editing methods typically rely on a 2D-lifting strategy, where individual views are edited independently and then lifted back into 3D space. This indirect pipeline often leads to blurry textures and inconsistent geometry, as 2D editors lack the spatial awareness required to preserve structure across viewpoints. To address these limitations, we propose VGGT-Edit, a feed-forward framework for text-conditioned native 3D scene editing. VGGT-Edit introduces depth-synchronized text injection to align semantic guidance with the backbone's spatial poses, ensuring stable instruction grounding. This semantic signal is then processed by a residual transformation head, which directly predicts 3D geometric displacements to deform the scene while preserving background stability. To ensure high-fidelity results, we supervise the framework with a multi-term objective function that enforces geometric accuracy and cross-view consistency. We also construct the DeltaScene Dataset, a large-scale dataset generated through an automated pipeline with 3D agreement filtering to ensure ground-truth quality. Experiments show that VGGT-Edit substantially outperforms 2D-lifting baselines, producing sharper object details, stronger multi-view consistency, and near-instant inference speed.

</details>


### [117] [RAVEN: Real-time Autoregressive Video Extrapolation with Consistency-model GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15190)
*Yanzuo Lu,Ronglai Zuo,Jiankang Deng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出RAVEN训练框架和CM-GRPO强化学习方法，解决自回归视频扩散模型在长时生成中因历史分布偏移导致的质量下降问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归视频扩散模型在训练和推理时存在历史分布偏移（history distribution gap），限制了长时生成质量。

Method: 1) RAVEN：将训练中的自回归回滚重新组织为干净历史端点与含噪去噪状态的交错序列，使训练注意力对齐推理时的外推过程，同时允许下游块损失监督历史表示。2) CM-GRPO：将一致性采样步骤重写为条件高斯转移，直接对核应用在线强化学习，避免Euler-Maruyama辅助过程。

Result: RAVEN在质量、语义和动态程度评估上超越最新因果视频蒸馏基线；CM-GRPO与RAVEN结合可进一步提升性能。

Conclusion: 通过训练-测试分布对齐和基于一致性模型的在线RL，有效提升了自回归视频扩散模型的长时生成质量。

Abstract: Causal autoregressive video diffusion models support real-time streaming generation by extrapolating future chunks from previously generated content. Distilling such generators from high-fidelity bidirectional teachers yields competitive few-step models, yet a persistent gap between the history distributions encountered during training and those arising at inference constrains generation quality over long horizons. We introduce the Real-time Autoregressive Video Extrapolation Network (RAVEN), a training-time test framework that repacks each self rollout into an interleaved sequence of clean historical endpoints and noisy denoising states. This formulation aligns training attention with inference-time extrapolation and allows downstream chunk losses to supervise the history representations on which future predictions depend. We further propose Consistency-model Group Relative Policy Optimization (CM-GRPO), which reformulates a consistency sampling step as a conditional Gaussian transition and applies online Reinforcement Learning (RL) directly to this kernel, avoiding the Euler-Maruyama auxiliary process adopted in prior flow-model RL formulations. Experiments demonstrate that RAVEN surpasses recent causal video distillation baselines across quality, semantic, and dynamic degree evaluations, and that CM-GRPO provides further gains when combined with RAVEN.

</details>


### [118] [Aligning Latent Geometry for Spherical Flow Matching in Image Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15193)
*Tuna Han Salih Meral,Kaan Oktay,Hidir Yesiltepe,Adil Kaan Akan,Pinar Yanardag*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于球面线性插值的潜在流匹配方法，通过将图像潜在表示从高斯噪声到VAE潜在空间的线性路径改为球面上的测地线路径，显著提升了图像生成质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有潜在流匹配方法在欧几里得线性路径上传输高斯噪声到VAE潜在空间时，两端点集中在薄球壳上，弦路径会离开球壳，导致不必要的径向分量干扰生成质量。

Method: 将每个潜在令牌分解为径向和角度分量；将数据潜在投影到固定令牌半径上，使用高斯噪声的径向投影作为球面先验；冻结编码器微调解码器；用球面线性插值替代线性插值，使得路径完全在球面上且速度目标纯角度化。

Result: 在类条件ImageNet-256上，该方法在不同图像分词器下一致改进了FID指标，不改变扩散架构，无需辅助编码器或表示对齐目标。

Conclusion: 通过将生成路径限制在球面上，消除了不必要的径向分量，实现了更高效的生成过程，且方法简洁通用。

Abstract: Latent flow matching for image generation usually transports Gaussian noise to variational autoencoder latents along linear paths. Both endpoints, however, concentrate in thin spherical shells, and a Euclidean chord leaves those shells even when preprocessing aligns their radii. By decomposing each latent token into radial and angular components, we show through component-swap probes that decoded perceptual and semantic content is carried predominantly by direction, with radius contributing much less. We therefore project data latents onto a fixed token radius, use the radial projection of Gaussian noise as the spherical prior, finetune the decoder with the encoder frozen, and replace linear interpolation with spherical linear interpolation. The resulting geodesic paths stay on the sphere at every timestep, and their velocity targets are purely angular by construction. Under matched training, the method consistently improves class-conditional ImageNet-256 FID across different image tokenizers, leaves the diffusion architecture unchanged, and requires no auxiliary encoder or representation-alignment objective.

</details>


### [119] [EntityBench: Towards Entity-Consistent Long-Range Multi-Shot Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.15199)
*Ruozhen He,Meng Wei,Ziyan Yang,Vicente Ordonez*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: EntityBench是一个基于真实叙事媒体的多镜头视频生成评测基准，包含140个片段（2,491个镜头），并配套提出EntityMem记忆增强生成系统，实验表明跨镜头实体一致性随递归距离显著下降，而显式逐实体记忆方法在角色保真度上表现最优（Cohen's d = +2.33）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测通常使用独立生成的提示集，实体覆盖有限且一致性指标简单，难以进行标准化比较。多镜头视频生成中保持角色、物体和地点的一致性仍是一个长期挑战。

Method: 构建EntityBench基准（140个片段、2,491个镜头，含显式逐镜头实体调度表，分easy/medium/hard三档）；提出EntityMem记忆增强生成系统，在生成前将验证过的逐实体视觉参考存储到持久记忆库中；设计三支柱评测套件（帧内质量、提示遵循对齐、跨镜头一致性）并设置保真度门控。

Result: 跨镜头实体一致性随递归距离增大而显著下降；EntityMem在角色保真度（Cohen's d = +2.33）和存在性方面优于现有方法。

Conclusion: 显式的逐实体记忆机制能有效提升多镜头视频生成中的跨镜头一致性，尤其是角色保真度；现有方法在处理长距离递归实体时表现不佳，亟需更好的解决方案。

Abstract: Multi-shot video generation extends single-shot generation to coherent visual narratives, yet maintaining consistent characters, objects, and locations across shots remains a challenge over long sequences. Existing evaluations typically use independently generated prompt sets with limited entity coverage and simple consistency metrics, making standardized comparison difficult. We introduce EntityBench, a benchmark of 140 episodes (2,491 shots) derived from real narrative media, with explicit per-shot entity schedules tracking characters, objects, and locations simultaneously across easy / medium / hard tiers of up to 50 shots, 13 cross-shot characters, 8 cross-shot locations, 22 cross-shot objects, and recurrence gaps spanning up to 48 shots. It is paired with a three-pillar evaluation suite that disentangles intra-shot quality, prompt-following alignment, and cross-shot consistency, with a fidelity gate that admits only accurate entity appearances into cross-shot scoring. As a baseline, we propose EntityMem, a memory-augmented generation system that stores verified per-entity visual references in a persistent memory bank before generation begins. Experiments show that cross-shot entity consistency degrades sharply with recurrence distance in existing methods, and that explicit per-entity memory yields the highest character fidelity (Cohen's d = +2.33) and presence among methods evaluated. Code and data are available at https://github.com/Catherine-R-He/EntityBench/.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [120] [Merging Methods for Multilingual Knowledge Editing for Large Language Models: An Empirical Odyssey](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.13919)
*Kunil Lee,Ki-Young Shin,Jong-Hyeok Lee,Young-Joo Suh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了向量合并方法在多语言知识编辑中的应用，发现共享协方差的向量求和是最可靠的策略，而简单求和效果差；任务奇异向量合并（TSVM）缓解多语言干扰的能力有限；权重缩放因子和秩压缩比对性能敏感，较大的缩放和较低的秩通常效果更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多语言知识编辑中语言特定的编辑会相互干扰，即使定位-编辑方法在单语言环境下表现良好，多语言场景下的干扰问题仍未解决。本文旨在探究向量合并方法对多语言知识编辑的有效性、TSVM减少多语言干扰的能力，以及权重缩放因子和秩压缩比的影响。

Method: 在MzsRE基准上，使用两种主流大语言模型（LLMs）、两种基础知识编辑方法和12种语言，在大规模批量编辑设置下评估六种向量合并变体，并分析其对性能的影响。

Result: 共享协方差的向量求和是最可靠的总体策略；简单求和（无共享协方差）表现不佳；TSVM在某些设置下能提升性能，但缓解多语言干扰的能力有限；性能对权重缩放因子和秩压缩比敏感，大于默认的缩放和相对较低的秩通常能带来更好结果。

Conclusion: 阐明了当前向量合并方法在多语言知识编辑中的实际优势和局限，为未来多语言知识编辑研究提供了指导。

Abstract: Multilingual knowledge editing (MKE) remains challenging because language-specific edits interfere with one another, even when locate-then-edit methods work well in monolingual settings. This paper focuses on three issues: the effectiveness of vector merging methods for MKE, the extent to which Task Singular Vectors for Merging (TSVM) can reduce multilingual interference, and the influence of the weight scaling factor and rank compression ratio on performance. We evaluate six merging variants with two popular backbone large language models, two base knowledge editing methods, and 12 languages on the MzsRE benchmark under a large-scale batch-editing setting. Our results show that vector summation with shared covariance is the most reliable overall strategy, whereas simple summation without shared covariance performs poorly. TSVM improves performance in some settings, but its ability to mitigate multilingual interference is limited. We also find that performance is sensitive to both weight scale and rank ratio, with larger-than-default scaling and relatively low rank often yielding better results. These findings clarify the practical strengths and limits of current vector merging methods for MKE and provide guidance for future multilingual knowledge editing research.

</details>


### [121] [Mistletoe: Stealthy Acceleration-Collapse Attacks on Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14005)
*Shuoyang Sun,Chang Da,Hao Fang,Kuofeng Gao,Xinhao Zhong,Yi Sun,Fan Mo,Shu-Tao Xia,Bin Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Mistletoe是一种针对推测解码的隐蔽加速崩溃攻击，通过利用草案模型与目标模型之间的不匹配，在保持输出质量的同时大幅降低接受长度和推理速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推测解码加速方法依赖草案模型与目标模型之间的近似匹配，但这种近似不可避免存在缺陷，形成了隐藏的攻击面。作者旨在揭示这一机制级漏洞并展示其严重后果。

Method: 通过联合优化两个目标：1) 降低草案-目标一致性的退化目标；2) 约束目标模型输出分布的语义保持目标。为解决目标冲突，引入零空间投影机制，将退化梯度投影到偏离局部语义保持方向的方向上。

Result: 在多种推测解码系统上的实验表明，Mistletoe显著降低了平均接受长度τ，导致加速比崩溃，降低了平均token吞吐量，同时保持输出质量和困惑度。

Conclusion: 推测解码引入了超越传统输出鲁棒性的机制级攻击面，现有加速系统需要更鲁棒的设计来防御此类攻击。

Abstract: Speculative decoding has become a widely adopted technique for accelerating large language model (LLM) inference by drafting multiple candidate tokens and verifying them with a target model in parallel. Its efficiency, however, critically depends on the average accepted length $τ$, i.e., how many draft tokens survive each verification step. In this work, we identify a new mechanism-level vulnerability in model-based speculative decoding: the drafter is trained to approximate the target model distribution, but this approximation is inevitably imperfect. Such a drafter-target mismatch creates a hidden attack surface where small perturbations can preserve the target model's visible behavior while substantially reducing draft-token acceptability. We propose Mistletoe, a stealthy acceleration-collapse attack against speculative decoding. Mistletoe directly targets the acceptance mechanism of speculative decoding. It jointly optimizes a degradation objective that decreases drafter-target agreement and a semantic-preservation objective that constrains the target model's output distribution. To resolve the conflict between these objectives, we introduce a null-space projection mechanism, where degradation gradients are projected away from the local semantic-preserving direction, suppressing draft acceptance while minimizing semantic drift. Experiments on various speculative decoding systems show that Mistletoe substantially reduces average accepted length $τ$, collapses speedup, and lowers averaged token throughput, while preserving output quality and perplexity. Our work highlights that speculative decoding introduces a mechanism-level attack surface beyond existing output robustness, calling for more robust designs of LLM acceleration systems.

</details>


### [122] [Physics-R1: An Audited Olympiad Corpus and Recipe for Visual Physics Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14040)
*Shan Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统审计了多模态物理评估流程中的三个隐藏问题：训练-评估数据污染、翻译漂移和多选饱和，并发布了四个新数据集（PhysCorp-A、PhysR1Corp、PhysOlym-A）和Physics-R1训练配方。Physics-R1在全新物理竞赛题评估上比8B基座提升+18.3 pp，接近Sonnet 4.5水平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态物理推理评估中存在未被检测的系统性问题（数据污染、翻译偏差、MCQ饱和），导致领域对视觉-语言推理能力的测量失真。

Method: (1) 三阶段审计流水线：Jaccard → mxbai-embed-large余弦相似度 → Haiku-4.5 LLM评判；(2) 爱沙尼亚语-英语双语对比实验；(3) MCQ与开放式评估格式对比；(4) 发布四个新资源并采用GSPO+DAPO强化学习训练Physics-R1。

Result: (1) SciInstruct中发现134个近重复和4846个释义候选；(2) 同一模型在英/爱沙尼亚语版本上存在17个百分点的准确率差异；(3) 同一权重在MCQ与开放式评估上差距达46个百分点；(4) Physics-R1在PhysOlym-A上达26.3%（+18.3 pp），在PhysReason上超越Qwen3-VL-32B和Gemini 2.5 Pro。

Conclusion: 现有物理评估存在严重的系统偏差，新构建的审计数据集和Physics-R1配方为可靠评估和训练提供了更坚实的基础。

Abstract: We audit the multimodal-physics evaluation pipeline end-to-end and document three undetected construction practices that distort how the field measures vision-language reasoning: train-eval contamination, translation drift, and MCQ saturation. (1) Public training pools (UGPhysics-Train, SciInstruct, MMK12) pass single-stage 5-gram-Jaccard audits with zero hits across all six public physics evals; a three-stage audit (Jaccard -> mxbai-embed-large cosine -> Haiku-4.5 LLM-judge) surfaces 134 near-duplicates and 4,846 paraphrase candidates in SciInstruct alone. (2) A 17-pp Sonnet 4.5 delta on 59 paired Estonian-English olympiad problems (30.5% vs. 13.6%; sign test p=0.011, McNemar p=0.021, paired bootstrap 95% CI [+5.1, +28.9] pp). (3) A 46-pp format-and-novelty gradient on identical Sonnet weights between MCQ (79.7% on PhyX) and open-ended olympiad evaluation (33.4% on PhysOlym-A). We release four artifacts addressing these gaps: PhysCorp-A (6,432-record three-stage-audited multimodal corpus), PhysR1Corp (2,268-record closed-form RL pool), PhysOlym-A (500-problem, 99.8% novel-source held-out olympiad eval with native difficulty labels and an EN/ET bilingual subset), and Physics-R1, a reference GSPO+DAPO recipe cold-started from Qwen3-VL-8B-Thinking. Across 3 seeds, Physics-R1 lifts the audited corpus over the 8B base by +18.3 pp on PhysOlym-A liberal (8.0 -> 26.3 +/- 1.7; 7.1 pp behind Sonnet 4.5), +15.7 pp on PhysReason (23.9 -> 39.6 +/- 6.4; ahead of Qwen3-VL-32B and Gemini 2.5 Pro), +6.9 pp on OlympiadBench-Physics (46.2 +/- 1.5), and +4.1 pp on PhyX MCQ (77.8 +/- 0.3).

</details>


### [123] [Derivation Prompting: A Logic-Based Method for Improving Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14053)
*Ignacio Sastre,Guillermo Moncecchi,Aiala Rosá*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出Derivation Prompting技术，通过构建可解释的推导树来增强RAG框架的生成步骤，在领域特定问答中减少不可接受回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在知识密集型领域特定问答任务中存在幻觉和错误推理问题。

Method: 基于逻辑推导思想，通过系统应用预定义规则从初始假设推导结论，构建可解释的推导树以控制生成过程。

Result: 在案例研究中，相比传统RAG和长上下文窗口方法，显著减少了不可接受回答的比例。

Conclusion: Derivation Prompting能有效增强生成过程的可控性和可解释性，减少LLM在领域特定问答中的错误。

Abstract: The application of Large Language Models to Question Answering has shown great promise, but important challenges such as hallucinations and erroneous reasoning arise when using these models, particularly in knowledge-intensive, domain-specific tasks. To address these issues, we introduce Derivation Prompting, a novel prompting technique for the generation step of the Retrieval-Augmented Generation framework. Inspired by logic derivations, this method involves deriving conclusions from initial hypotheses through the systematic application of predefined rules. It constructs a derivation tree that is interpretable and adds control over the generation process. We applied this method in a specific case study, significantly reducing unacceptable answers compared to traditional RAG and long-context window methods.

</details>


### [124] [PEML: Parameter-efficient Multi-Task Learning with Optimized Continuous Prompts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14055)
*Anjir Ahmed Chowdhury,Syed Zawad,Xiaolong Ma,Xu Dong,Feng Yan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为PEML的参数高效多任务学习方法，通过联合优化连续提示和低秩模型权重适配，在多个基准测试上平均准确率提升最高达6.67%，单个任务最高提升10.75%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PEFT方法（如LoRA、Prefix Tuning）主要针对单任务设计，缺乏对多任务学习中提示优化与模型适配的协同考虑，而多任务微调能共享公共特征、减少数据需求并节约资源。

Method: 提出PEML框架，采用神经架构工程方法优化连续提示，同时进行模型权重的低秩适配，实现提示优化与模型适配的联合优化。

Result: 在GLUE、SuperGLUE、MMLU和常识推理等基准上，与MTL-LoRA、MultiLoRa、C-Poly、MoE等SOTA方法相比，平均准确率提升最高达6.67%，单个任务峰值提升10.75%。

Conclusion: PEML通过联合优化提示和模型权重，有效提升了多任务学习的参数高效微调性能。

Abstract: Parameter-Efficient Fine-Tuning (PEFT) is widely used for adapting Large Language Models (LLMs) for various tasks. Recently, there has been an increasing demand for fine-tuning a single LLM for multiple tasks because it requires overall less data for fine-tuning thanks to the common features shared among tasks. More importantly, LLMs are resource demanding and deploying a single model for multiple tasks facilitates resource consolidation and consumes significantly less resources compared to deploying individual large model for each task. Existing PEFT methods like LoRA and Prefix Tuning are designed to adapt LLMs to a specific task. LoRA and its variation focus on aligning the model itself for tasks, overlooking the importance of prompt tuning in multi-task learning while Prefix Tuning only adopts a simple architecture to optimize prompts, which limits the adaption capabilities for multi-task. To enable efficient fine-tuning for multi-task learning, it is important to co-optimize prompt optimization and model adaptation. In this work, we propose a Parameter-Efficient Multi-task Learning (\PM), which employs a neural architecture engineering method for optimizing the continuous prompts while also performing low-rank adaption for model weights. We prototype PEML by creating an automated framework for optimizing the continuous prompts and adapting model weights. We evaluate PEML against state-of-the-arts multi-task learning methods MTL-LoRA, MultiLoRa, C-Poly, and MoE, on the GLUE, SuperGLUE, Massive Multitask Language Understanding, and commonsense reasoning benchmarks. The evaluation results present an average accuracy improvement of up to 6.67%, with individual tasks showing peak gains of up to 10.75%.

</details>


### [125] [When Evidence Conflicts: Uncertainty and Order Effects in Retrieval-Augmented Biomedical Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14115)
*Yikun Han,Mengfei Lan,Halil Kilicoglu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了生物医学检索增强大语言模型在面对冲突证据时的表现，发现仅改变证据顺序会导致11.4%-25.2%的预测翻转，并提出了一种结合模型置信度与证据冲突检测的弃权分数，在困难条件下选择性准确率提升7.2-33.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生物医学检索增强LLM的评估通常侧重于有帮助上下文下的答案准确性，而忽视了模型在面临不完整、误导性或内部矛盾的证据时的可靠性和鲁棒性问题。

Method: 构建HealthContradict数据集，在五种受控证据条件下评估六个开源LLM，包括无检索上下文、仅正确上下文、仅错误上下文以及两种包含正确与矛盾文档但顺序相反的混合条件。提出一种冲突感知弃权分数，结合模型置信度与证据冲突检测。

Result: 在冲突证据顺序对比中（相同文档仅顺序颠倒），所有模型准确率均下降，11.4%-25.2%的预测发生翻转。在仅错误条件（IC）和错误优先冲突条件（ICC）下，冲突感知弃权分数在75%、50%、25%覆盖率下，选择性准确率分别提升7.2-33.4和3.6-14.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 冲突的生物医学证据既是不确定性也是鲁棒性问题，评估和弃权方法应明确考虑证据不一致性。

Abstract: Biomedical retrieval-augmented large language models (LLMs) often face evidence that is incomplete, misleading, or internally contradictory, yet evaluation usually emphasizes answer accuracy under helpful context rather than reliability under conflict. Using HealthContradict, we evaluate six open-weight LLMs under five controlled evidence conditions: no retrieved context, correct-only context, incorrect-only context, and two mixed conditions containing both correct and contradictory documents in opposite orders. In this conflicting-evidence order contrast, where the same two documents are both present and only their order is reversed, accuracy drops for every model and 11.4%--25.2% of predictions flip. To support abstention in these difficult cases, we also evaluate a conflict-aware abstention score that combines model confidence with a detector of evidence conflict. In the two hardest conditions, this score improves selective accuracy over confidence-only, with mean gains of 7.2--33.4 points in incorrect-only (`IC') and 3.6--14.4 points in incorrect-first conflicting (`ICC') conditions across 75%, 50%, and 25% coverage. These results show that conflicting biomedical evidence is both an uncertainty and robustness problem and motivate evaluation and abstention methods that explicitly account for evidence disagreement.

</details>


### [126] [Generative Floor Plan Design with LLMs via Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14117)
*Luis Lara,Aristides Milios,Zhi Hao Luo,Aditya Sharma,Ge Ya Luo,Christopher Beckham,Florian Golemo,Christopher Pal*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于文本的 floor plan 生成方法，通过对大语言模型进行微调并应用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR），使模型能够同时满足拓扑连接约束和数值约束（如房间尺寸和面积），在多个指标上显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有面向专业 floor plan 设计的生成方法主要支持房间之间的拓扑连接约束，但无法处理房间尺寸和面积等数值约束，限制了其在工程实践中的应用。

Method: 1) 在真实 floor plan 数据上微调大语言模型（LLM）；2) 应用基于可验证奖励的强化学习（RLVR）提高对拓扑和数值约束的遵守程度，同时惩罚无效或重叠输出；3) 设计一套约束遵守度量标准，系统评估生成结果与用户定义约束的一致性。

Result: 该方法生成满足用户定义连接性和数值约束的 floor plan，在真实性、兼容性和多样性指标上优于现有方法。在所有任务中，与现有方法相比，兼容性指标相对降低至少 94%。

Conclusion: LLM 能够有效处理 floor plan 生成中的约束条件，表明文本生成建模在该领域具有更广泛的应用前景。

Abstract: An AI system for professional floor plan design must precisely control room dimensions and areas while respecting the desired connectivity between rooms and maintaining functional and aesthetic quality. Existing generative approaches focus primarily on respecting the requested connectivity between rooms, but do not support generating floor plans that respect numerical constraints. We introduce a text-based floor plan generation approach that fine-tunes a large language model (LLM) on real plans and then applies reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) to improve adherence to topological and numerical constraints while discouraging invalid or overlapping outputs. Furthermore, we design a set of constraint adherence metrics to systematically measure how generated floor plans align with user-defined constraints. Our model generates floor plans that satisfy user-defined connectivity and numerical constraints and outperforms existing methods on Realism, Compatibility, and Diversity metrics. Across all tasks, our approach achieves at least a 94% relative reduction in Compatibility compared with existing methods. Our results demonstrate that LLMs can effectively handle constraints in this setting, suggesting broader applications for text-based generative modeling.

</details>


### [127] [ROK-FORTRESS: Measuring the Effect of Geopolitical Transcreation for National Security and Public Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14152)
*Michael S. Lee,Yash Maurya,Drew Rein,Bert Herring,Jonathan Nguyen,Kyungho Song,Udari Madhushani Sehwag,Jiyeon Cho,Kaustubh Deshpande,Yeongkyun Jang,Jiyeon Joo,Minn Seok Choi,Evi Fuelle,Christina Q Knight,Joseph Brandifino,Max Fenkell*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ROK-FORTRESS基准，通过“转创矩阵”分离语言与地缘政治背景对LLM安全行为的影响，发现韩语版本存在一致性抑制效应，且韩语地缘背景能缓解该抑制，揭示了翻译-only评估无法捕捉的交互现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM安全评估多依赖翻译-only基准，无法区分语言和地缘政治背景对安全行为的独立影响，且实证证据局限于少量语言对，缺乏对高风险国家安全与公共安全场景中跨语言与跨文化交互的系统研究。

Method: 构建英-韩双语、美-韩地缘政治轴的NSPS基准ROK-FORTRESS；采用“转创矩阵”设计，在语言（英语vs韩语）和地缘背景（美国vs韩国实体、机构、操作细节）两个维度上组合控制；每个对抗性提示配备双用途良性对照以量化过度拒答；使用校准的LLM-as-a-judge面板结合专家构建的二元评分标准评估模型响应。

Result: 在前沿模型和韩语优化模型中，韩语版本均存在一致性安全抑制效应；不同模型在地缘背景与语言的交互上存在显著差异；多数模型中韩语地缘背景能缓解韩语驱动的抑制效应，且无模型表现出相反方向的显著放大。

Conclusion: 在英-韩案例中，LLM安全行为受到语言作为风险信号与上下文交互的塑造，这是翻译-only评估无法捕获的；转创矩阵方法设计具有推广到其他语言-文化对的泛化能力。

Abstract: Safety evaluations for large language models (LLMs) increasingly target high-stakes National Security and Public Safety (NSPS) risks, yet multilingual safety is typically assessed through translation-only benchmarks that preserve the underlying scenario, and empirical evidence of how language and geopolitical context interact remains limited to a narrow set of language pairs. We introduce \emph{ROK-FORTRESS} https://huggingface.co/datasets/ScaleAI/ROK-FORTRESS_public, a bilingual, culturally adversarial NSPS benchmark that uses the English--Korean language pair and U.S.--ROK geopolitical axis as a case study, separating the effects of language and geopolitical grounding via a \emph{transcreation matrix}: adversarial intents are evaluated under controlled combinations of (i) English versus Korean language and (ii) U.S.\ versus Korean entities, institutions, and operational details. Each adversarial prompt is paired with a dual-use benign counterpart to quantify over-refusal. Model responses are then scored using calibrated LLM-as-a-judge panels, applying our expert-crafted, prompt-specific binary rubrics.
  Across a dual-track set of frontier and Korean-optimized models, we find a consistent suppression effect in Korean variants and substantial model-to-model variation in how geopolitical grounding interacts with language. In many models, Korean grounding mitigates the Korean language-driven suppression -- with no model showing significant amplification in the other direction -- indicating that, at least in the English--Korean case, safety behavior is shaped by language-as-risk signals and context interactions that translation-only evaluations miss. The transcreation matrix methodology is designed to generalize to other language--culture pairs.

</details>


### [128] [BOOKMARKS: Efficient Active Storyline Memory for Role-playing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14169)
*Letian Peng,Ziche Liu,Yiming Huang,Longfei Yun,Kun Zhou,Yupeng Hou,Jingbo Shang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出BOOKMARKS，一种基于搜索的角色扮演智能体记忆框架，通过主动维护任务相关的"书签"（问题-答案结构）替代传统循环摘要，在85个角色上显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RPA记忆方法（如profiling）依赖循环摘要，压缩过程会丢失重要细节，导致长程一致性不足。

Method: BOOKMARKS框架：1) 将记忆组织为书签（特定故事节点的问答对）；2) 对当前任务主动选择可重用书签或初始化新书签；3) 同步更新书签答案至当前故事点；4) 支持概念、行为和状态三类搜索，各配高效同步方法。

Result: 在16个文本来源的85个角色上显著超越现有RPA记忆基线方法，验证了基于搜索的记忆机制的有效性。

Conclusion: 主动搜索式记忆比被动循环摘要更能保留任务关键细节，同时通过被动更新避免不必要计算，为RPA长程一致性提供更优方案。

Abstract: Memory systems are critical for role-playing agents (RPAs) to maintain long-horizon consistency. However, existing RPA memory methods (e.g., profiling) mainly rely on recurrent summarization, whose compression inevitably discards important details. To address this issue, we propose a search-based memory framework called BOOKMARKS, which actively initializes, maintains, and updates task-relevant pieces of bookmarks for the current task (e.g., character acting). A bookmark is structured as the answer to a question at a specific point in the storyline. For each current task, BOOKMARKS selects reusable existing bookmarks or initializes new ones (at storyline beginning) with useful questions. These bookmarks are then synchronized to the current story point, with their answers updated accordingly, so they can be efficiently reused in future grounding rounds. Compared with recurrent summarization, BOOKMARKS offers (1) active grounding for capturing task-specific details and (2) passive updating to avoid unnecessary computation. In implementation, BOOKMARKS supports concept, behavior, and state searches, each powered by an efficient synchronization method. BOOKMARKS significantly outperforms RPA memory baselines on 85 characters from 16 artifacts, demonstrating the effectiveness of search-based memory for RPAs.

</details>


### [129] [Why Retrieval-Augmented Generation Fails: A Graph Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14192)
*Kai Guo,Xinnan Dai,Zhibo Zhang,Nuohan Lin,Shenglai Zeng,Jie Ren,Haoyu Han,Jiliang Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过构建归因图（attribution graphs）对检索增强生成（RAG）系统进行模型内部研究，发现正确预测与错误预测在推理路径深度、证据流分布和局部连接结构上存在显著差异，并基于此开发了图结构错误检测框架和针对性干预方法，以提升RAG系统的准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管RAG通过检索外部证据提升了LLM的性能，但系统仍频繁产生错误答案，而错误发生的内部机制尚不明确。本文旨在从模型内部理解RAG失败的原因，即为什么在拥有外部信息的情况下仍产生错误。

Method: 1）使用电路追踪（circuit tracing）构建归因图，建模Transformer解码过程中检索证据、中间激活和生成token之间的信息流；2）在多个QA基准上对比正确与错误预测的图结构差异；3）基于图拓扑特征开发错误检测框架；4）通过归因图指导的针对性干预（增强问题约束的证据接地）重塑内部路由。

Result: 正确预测表现出更深的推理路径、更分散的证据流和更结构化的局部连接模式；错误预测则呈现更浅、更碎片化且过度集中的证据流。基于此，错误检测和干预方法有效减少了RAG系统的错误。

Conclusion: 归因图为理解RAG内部机制提供了电路级视角，图结构特征可用于错误检测和针对性干预，从而提升检索信息与生成过程的整合质量。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) has become a powerful and widely used approach for improving large language models by grounding generation in retrieved evidence. However, RAG systems still produce incorrect answers in many cases. Why RAG fails despite having access to external information remains poorly understood. We present a model-internal study of retrieval-augmented generation that examines how retrieved evidence influences answer generation. Using circuit tracing, we construct attribution graphs that model the flow of information through transformer layers during decoding. These graphs represent interactions among retrieved context, intermediate model activations, and generated tokens, providing a graph, circuit-level view of how external evidence is integrated into the model's reasoning process across multiple question answering benchmarks, we observe consistent structural differences: correct predictions exhibit deeper reasoning paths, more distributed evidence flow, and a more structured pattern of local connectivity, while failed predictions show shallower, fragmented, and overly concentrated evidence flow. Building on these findings, we develop a graph-based error detection framework that uses attribution-graph topology features. Furthermore, we show that attribution graphs enable targeted interventions. By reinforcing question-constrained evidence grounding, we reshape internal routing so that answer generation remains guided by the question, leading to more effective integration of retrieved information and fewer errors.

</details>


### [130] [GradShield: Alignment Preserving Finetuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14194)
*Zhanhao Hu,Xiao Huang,Patrick Mendoza,Emad A. Alghamdi,Basel Alomair,Raluca Ada Popa,David Wagner*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GradShield是一种基于梯度计算的过滤方法，通过为每个数据点计算微调隐式有害性分数(FIHS)并采用自适应阈值算法，在微调前识别并移除有害数据，从而保护LLM的安全对齐，在保持ASR低于6%的同时维持模型效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在微调后存在安全对齐被破坏的重大风险，因为模型可能被显式或隐式的有害数据所妥协，甚至一些看似良性的数据也可能无意中导致模型出现失调行为，因此需要一种有效的过滤方法来保护模型安全。

Method: 提出GradShield过滤方法，通过计算每个数据点的微调隐式有害性分数(FIHS)来量化数据的有害程度，并采用自适应阈值算法自动确定过滤边界，在微调前移除潜在有害数据点。

Result: 在多个效用微调任务和不同有害数据比例下，GradShield在所有基线方法中表现最优，能够持续保持攻击成功率(ASR)低于6%，同时不损害模型的效用性能。

Conclusion: GradShield作为一种 principled 的过滤方法，能够有效保护LLM在微调过程中的安全对齐，为安全微调提供了可靠的解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) pose a significant risk of safety misalignment after finetuning, as models can be compromised by both explicitly and implicitly harmful data. Even some seemingly benign data can inadvertently steer a model towards misaligned behaviors. To address this, we introduce GradShield, a principled filtering method that safeguards LLMs during finetuning by identifying and removing harmful data points before they corrupt the model's alignment. It removes potentially harmful data by computing a Finetuning Implicit Harmfulness Score (FIHS) for each data point and employs an adaptive thresholding algorithm. We apply GradShield to multiple utility fine-tuning tasks across varying levels of harmful data and evaluate the safety and utility performance of the resulting LLMs using various metrics. The results show that GradShield outperforms all baseline methods, consistently maintaining an Attack Success Rate (ASR) below $6\%$ while preserving utility performance.

</details>


### [131] [What Makes Words Hard? Sakura at BEA 2026 Shared Task on Vocabulary Difficulty Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14257)
*Adam Nohejl,Xuanxin Wu,Yusuke Ide,Maria Angelica Riera Machin,Yi-Ning Chang,Hitomi Yanaka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种词汇难度预测模型：基于LLM微调的黑盒模型（r>0.91，在开放赛道中取得最佳成绩）和可解释模型（r>0.77）。研究发现英国文化协会知识型词汇列表（KVL）中项目的难度不仅受词汇本身产出难度影响，还受拼写难度和测试题构造的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有词汇难度预测方法缺乏高精度与可解释性的平衡，且对词汇难度影响因素（如拼写、题目构造）的系统分析不足。

Method: 1) 黑盒模型：对LLM进行微调，采用软目标损失函数（soft-target loss function）以适应评分任务；2) 可解释模型：在保持较高相关性的同时提供影响难度的因素分析。

Result: 黑盒模型相关系数r>0.91，在开放赛道中排名第一；可解释模型相关系数r>0.77，优于微调编码器基线。

Conclusion: KVL词汇难度受拼写难度和测试题构造的显著影响，而不仅仅是词汇本身的产出难度。代码已开源。

Abstract: We describe two types of models for vocabulary difficulty prediction: a high-accuracy black-box model, which achieved the top shared task result in the open track, and an explainable model, which outperforms a fine-tuned encoder baseline. As the black-box model, we fine-tuned an LLM using a soft-target loss function for effective application to the rating task, achieving r > 0.91. The explainable model provides insights into what impacts the difficulty of each item while maintaining a strong correlation (r > 0.77). We further analyze the results, demonstrating that the difficulty of items in the British Council's Knowledge-based Vocabulary Lists (KVL) is often affected by spelling difficulty or the construction of the test items, in addition to the genuine production difficulty of the words. We make our code available online at https://github.com/adno/vocabulary-difficulty .

</details>


### [132] [Ideology Prediction of German Political Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14352)
*Sinclair Schneider,Florian Steuber,Joao A. G. Schneider,Gabi Dreo Rodosek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出基于Transformer的模型，可将德语政治文本的倾向映射到连续左右频谱（-1到1），在多个测试集上表现良好（DeBERTa-large F1=0.844, Gemma2-2B MAE=0.172），证明模型架构和领域数据与模型规模同等重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为更好地理解从极左到极右各政治运动的修辞特征，需要一种能连续量化文本政治倾向的模型，使分析人员可以聚焦特定政治光谱段（如保守派），而传统多类分类器只能依赖预设类别。

Method: 从13个候选Transformer中选择最优基础模型；构建四个语料库（德国联邦议院注释笔记、Wahl-O-Mat决策工具、33家报纸文章、597名议员共535,200条推文）；使用两个语料库训练、两个语料库测试以避免过拟合；输出归一化标量d∈[-1,1]表示政治倾向。

Result: DeBERTa-large在域内测试F1=0.844，推文域外测试ACC=0.864；Gemma2-2B在报纸域外测试MAE=0.172；模型在德语新闻上的政治倾向识别达到民意调查水平。

Conclusion: Transformer模型可有效识别德语文本的政治倾向；模型架构和领域特定训练数据量与模型规模对政治偏见估计同等重要；讨论了方法论局限并提出了改进方向。

Abstract: Elections represent a crucial milestone in a nation's ongoing development. To better understand the political rhetoric from various movements, ranging from left to right, we propose a transformer-based model capable of projecting the political orientation of a text on a continuous left-to-right spectrum, represented by a normalized scalar d between -1 and 1. This approach enables analysts to focus on specific segments of the political landscape, such as conservatives, while excluding liberal and far-right movements. Such a task can only be achieved with multiclass classifiers, provided that the desired orientation is incorporated within one of their predefined classes. To determine the most suitable foundation model among 13 candidate transformers for this task, we constructed four distinct corpora. One corpus comprised annotated plenary notes from the German Bundestag, while another was based on an official online decision-making tool, Wahl-O-Mat. The third corpus consisted of articles from 33 newspapers, each identified by its political orientation, and the fourth included 535,200 tweets from 597 members of the 20th and 21st German Bundestag. To mitigate overfitting, we used two distinct corpora for training and two for testing, respectively. For in-domain performance, DeBERTa-large achieved the highest F1 score F1=0.844 as well as for the X (Twitter) out-of-domain test ACC=0.864. Regarding the newspaper out-of-domain test, Gemma2-2B excelled (MAE = 0.172). This study demonstrates that transformer models can recognize political framing in German news at the level of public opinion polls. Our findings suggest that both the model architecture and the availability of domain-specific training data can be as influential as model size for estimating political bias. We discuss methodological limitations and outline directions for improving the robustness of bias measurement.

</details>


### [133] [LLM-based Detection of Manipulative Political Narratives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14354)
*Sinclair Schneider,Florian Steuber,Gabi Dreo Rodosek*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种结合提示过滤与无监督聚类的计算框架，用于检测和结构化社交媒体中的操纵性政治叙事，在120万条帖子中识别出41个不同叙事簇。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 政治讨论向社交媒体转移使得操纵性政治叙事泛滥，而区分操纵性叙事与合法批评是一大挑战，现有方法缺乏自动化和结构化能力。

Method: 1) 使用结合文献记载的竞选叙事与合法批评的详细少样本提示，让推理模型过滤标记操纵性帖子；2) 对剩余帖子进行嵌入与UMAP降维；3) 使用HDBSCAN进行无监督聚类发现叙事群组；4) 使用推理模型揭示每个簇背后的叙事。

Result: 在超过120万条社交媒体帖子中成功识别出41个不同的操纵性叙事簇。

Conclusion: 该框架通过将提示过滤与无监督聚类相结合，能够有效识别和结构化操纵性政治叙事，且不依赖预定义目标类别，具备发现新叙事簇的能力。

Abstract: We present a new computational framework for detecting and structuring manipulative political narratives. A task that became more important due to the shift of political discussions to social media. One of the primary challenges thereby is differentiating between manipulative political narratives and legitimate critiques. Some posts may also reframe actual events within a manipulative context.
  To achieve good clustering results, we filter manipulative posts beforehand using a detailed few-shot prompt that combines documented campaign narratives with legitimate criticisms to differentiate them. This prompt enables a reasoning model to assign labels, retaining only manipulative narrative posts for further processing.
  The remaining posts are subsequently embedded and dimensionality-reduced using UMAP, before HDBSCAN is applied to uncover narrative groups. A key advantage of this unsupervised approach is its independence from a predefined list of target categories, enabling it to uncover new narrative clusters.
  Finally, a reasoning model is employed to uncover the narrative behind each cluster. This approach, applied to over 1.2 million social media posts, effectively identified 41 distinct manipulative narrative clusters by integrating prompt-based filtering with unsupervised clustering.

</details>


### [134] [Reinforcement Learning with Semantic Rewards Enables Low-Resource Language Expansion without Alignment Tax](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14366)
*Zeli Su,Ziyin Zhang,Zhou Liu,Xuexian Song,Zhankai Xu,Longfei Zheng,Xiaolu Zhang,Rong Fu,Guixian Xu,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对大语言模型扩展到低资源语言时存在的"对齐税"问题（目标语言能力提升伴随通用能力灾难性遗忘），本文提出基于语义空间对齐的GRPO强化学习方法，用嵌入级语义奖励替代传统SFT的似然最大化，在藏汉翻译和藏语标题生成任务上验证了该方法能有效获取低资源能力同时减轻对齐税，保留通用能力，并学习到更可迁移的鲁棒表征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型扩展到低资源语言时存在"对齐税"问题，即通过SFT微调提升目标语言能力会导致通用能力灾难性遗忘。作者认为这一权衡源于SFT在狭窄且有偏的数据分布上强制执行token级表面模仿的刚性。

Method: 提出语义空间对齐范式，使用Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO)，以嵌入级语义奖励（而非似然最大化）优化模型，鼓励通过灵活实现保留语义，减少对预训练知识的破坏性干扰。

Result: 在藏汉机器翻译和藏语标题生成任务上，该方法能获取低资源语言能力同时显著减轻对齐税，比SFT更好地保留通用能力。尽管表面重叠较少，语义RL在开放生成中产生更高的语义质量和偏好，且少样本迁移结果表明其学习到更可迁移和鲁棒的表征。

Conclusion: 语义奖励的强化学习为低资源语言扩展提供了一条更安全、更可靠的路径。

Abstract: Extending large language models (LLMs) to low-resource languages often incurs an "alignment tax": improvements in the target language come at the cost of catastrophic forgetting in general capabilities. We argue that this trade-off arises from the rigidity of supervised fine-tuning (SFT), which enforces token-level surface imitation on narrow and biased data distributions. To address this limitation, we propose a semantic-space alignment paradigm powered by Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO), where the model is optimized using embedding-level semantic rewards rather than likelihood maximization. This objective encourages meaning preservation through flexible realizations, enabling controlled updates that reduce destructive interference with pretrained knowledge. We evaluate our approach on Tibetan-Chinese machine translation and Tibetan headline generation. Experiments show that our method acquires low-resource capabilities while markedly mitigating alignment tax, preserving general competence more effectively than SFT. Despite producing less rigid surface overlap, semantic RL yields higher semantic quality and preference in open-ended generation, and few-shot transfer results indicate that it learns more transferable and robust representations under limited supervision. Overall, our study demonstrates that reinforcement learning with semantic rewards provides a safer and more reliable pathway for inclusive low-resource language expansion.

</details>


### [135] [Mitigating Data Scarcity in Psychological Defense Classification with Context-Aware Synthetic Augmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.14380)
*Hoang-Thuy-Duong Vu,Quoc-Cuong Pham,Huy-Hieu Pham*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对心理防御机制文本分类中的数据稀缺和类别不平衡问题，提出结合上下文感知的合成数据增强与混合分类模型（融合上下文语言表征、临床特征及150个标注防御条目），在PsyDefDetect任务中达到58.26%准确率和24.62%宏F1，较基线提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 心理防御机制（PDMs）的自动分类具有临床价值，但面临数据稀缺和类别不平衡的严重挑战，而单纯的生成式增强方法缺乏心理学理论基础，无法有效解决这一问题。

Method: 提出上下文感知的合成增强框架（CASA），结合混合分类模型，该模型融合上下文语言表征、基础临床特征以及150个标注防御条目。通过提示质量（定义质量）控制生成保真度。
